1	/**
2	 * SKF Status Command
3	 * Shows installation state, version, tier, configured IDEs, and sidecar status.
4	 */
5	
6	const chalk = require('chalk');
7	const path = require('node:path');
8	const fs = require('fs-extra');
9	const yaml = require('js-yaml');
10	const { readManifest } = require('../lib/manifest');
11	const { getAvailablePlatforms } = require('../lib/ide-skills');
12	
13	const SKF_FOLDER = '_bmad/skf';
14	const SIDECAR_FOLDER = '_bmad/_memory/forger-sidecar';
15	
16	function getIdeNames() {
17	  const names = { other: 'Other' };
18	  for (const p of getAvailablePlatforms()) {
19	    names[p.value] = p.label;
20	  }
21	  return names;
22	}
23	
24	async function readYaml(filePath) {
25	  try {
26	    const content = await fs.readFile(filePath, 'utf8');
27	    return yaml.load(content) || {};
28	  } catch {
29	    return null;
30	  }
31	}
32	
33	async function getStatus(projectDir) {
34	  const skfDir = path.join(projectDir, SKF_FOLDER);
35	  const sidecarDir = path.join(projectDir, SIDECAR_FOLDER);
36	
37	  const installed = await fs.pathExists(skfDir);
38	  if (!installed) {
39	    return { installed: false };
40	  }
41	
42	  // Read config
43	  const config = await readYaml(path.join(skfDir, 'config.yaml'));
44	
45	  // Read forge tier
46	  const forgeTier = await readYaml(path.join(sidecarDir, 'forge-tier.yaml'));
47	
48	  // Read preferences
49	  const preferences = await readYaml(path.join(sidecarDir, 'preferences.yaml'));
50	
51	  // Check agent skill in module dir (always present when installed)
52	  const agentInstalled = await fs.pathExists(path.join(skfDir, 'skf-forger', 'SKILL.md'));
53	
54	  // Count workflow skills (skf-* directories with SKILL.md, excluding the forger agent)
55	  let workflowCount = 0;
56	  if (await fs.pathExists(skfDir)) {
57	    const entries = await fs.readdir(skfDir);
58	    for (const entry of entries) {
59	      if (!entry.startsWith('skf-') || entry === 'skf-forger') continue;
60	      const skillPath = path.join(skfDir, entry, 'SKILL.md');
61	      if (await fs.pathExists(skillPath)) {
62	        workflowCount++;
63	      }
64	    }
65	  }
66	
67	  // Check output folders
68	  const skillsFolder = config?.skills_output_folder || 'skills';
69	  const forgeDataFolder = config?.forge_data_folder || 'forge-data';
70	  const skillsFolderExists = await fs.pathExists(path.join(projectDir, skillsFolder));
71	  const forgeDataFolderExists = await fs.pathExists(path.join(projectDir, forgeDataFolder));
72	
73	  // Check sidecar state
74	  const sidecarExists = await fs.pathExists(sidecarDir);
75	  const tierDetected = forgeTier?.tier != null;
76	
77	  // Read manifest
78	  const manifest = await readManifest(projectDir);
79	
80	  return {
81	    installed: true,
82	    config,
83	    forgeTier,
84	    preferences,
85	    agentInstalled,
86	    workflowCount,
87	    skillsFolder,
88	    skillsFolderExists,
89	    forgeDataFolder,
90	    forgeDataFolderExists,
91	    sidecarExists,
92	    tierDetected,
93	    manifest,
94	  };
95	}
96	
97	function displayStatus(status, version) {
98	  console.log('');
99	  console.log(chalk.hex('#F59E0B').bold('  Skill Forge — Status'));
100	  console.log(chalk.dim(`  v${version}`));
101	  console.log('');
102	
103	  if (!status.installed) {
104	    console.log(chalk.yellow('  Not installed.'));
105	    console.log(chalk.dim('  Run: npx bmad-module-skill-forge install'));
106	    console.log('');
107	    return;
108	  }
109	
110	  const config = status.config || {};
111	
112	  // Installation
113	  const manifest = status.manifest;
114	  console.log(chalk.white.bold('  Installation'));
115	  console.log(`    Project:      ${chalk.hex('#FBBF24')(config.project_name || '(unknown)')}`);
116	  console.log(`    SKF folder:   ${chalk.dim(SKF_FOLDER + '/')}`);
117	  console.log(`    Agent:        ${status.agentInstalled ? chalk.green('installed') : chalk.yellow('not installed')}`);
118	  console.log(`    Skills:       ${chalk.white(status.workflowCount)}`);
119	  if (manifest) {
120	    console.log(
121	      `    Installed:    ${chalk.dim(manifest.installed_at ? new Date(manifest.installed_at).toLocaleDateString() : '(unknown)')}`,
122	    );
123	    console.log(`    Manifest:     ${chalk.green('present')}`);
124	  } else {
125	    console.log(`    Manifest:     ${chalk.yellow('missing')} ${chalk.dim('(reinstall to generate)')}`);
126	  }
127	  console.log('');
128	
129	  // IDEs
130	  const ides = config.ides || [];
131	  const ideNames = getIdeNames();
132	  console.log(chalk.white.bold('  IDEs'));
133	  if (ides.length > 0) {
134	    for (const ide of ides) {
135	      console.log(`    ${chalk.green('●')} ${ideNames[ide] || ide}`);
136	    }
137	  } else {
138	    console.log(chalk.dim('    None configured'));
139	  }
140	  console.log('');
141	
142	  // Forge Tier
143	  const ft = status.forgeTier || {};
144	  const tools = ft.tools || {};
145	  console.log(chalk.white.bold('  Forge Tier'));
146	  if (status.tierDetected) {
147	    const tierColors = { Quick: chalk.yellow, Forge: chalk.hex('#F59E0B'), 'Forge+': chalk.hex('#E88D0E'), Deep: chalk.hex('#FCD34D') };
148	    const tierColor = tierColors[ft.tier] || chalk.white;
149	    console.log(`    Tier:         ${tierColor(ft.tier)}`);
150	    console.log(`    Detected:     ${chalk.dim(ft.tier_detected_at || '(unknown)')}`);
151	  } else {
152	    console.log(chalk.dim('    Not detected yet — run @Ferris SF'));
153	  }
154	  console.log(`    ast-grep:     ${formatTool(tools.ast_grep)}`);
155	  console.log(`    gh CLI:       ${formatTool(tools.gh_cli)}`);
156	  console.log(`    ccc:          ${formatTool(tools.ccc)}`);
157	  console.log(`    QMD:          ${formatTool(tools.qmd)}`);
158	  console.log('');
159	
160	  // Output Folders
161	  console.log(chalk.white.bold('  Output Folders'));
162	  console.log(`    Skills:       ${status.skillsFolder}/ ${status.skillsFolderExists ? chalk.green('✓') : chalk.yellow('missing')}`);
163	  console.log(`    Forge data:   ${status.forgeDataFolder}/ ${status.forgeDataFolderExists ? chalk.green('✓') : chalk.yellow('missing')}`);
164	  console.log('');
165	
166	  // Sidecar
167	  console.log(chalk.white.bold('  Sidecar'));
168	  console.log(`    State:        ${status.sidecarExists ? chalk.green('initialized') : chalk.yellow('missing')}`);
169	  console.log(`    Location:     ${chalk.dim(SIDECAR_FOLDER + '/')}`);
170	  console.log('');
171	}
172	
173	function formatTool(value) {
174	  if (value === true || value === 'available') return chalk.green('available');
175	  if (value == null) return chalk.dim('not detected');
176	  return chalk.yellow(String(value));
177	}
178	
179	module.exports = {
180	  command: 'status',
181	  description: 'Show SKF installation state, version, tier, and configuration',
182	  options: [],
183	  action: async () => {
184	    try {
185	      const projectDir = process.cwd();
186	      const packageJson = require('../../../package.json');
187	      const status = await getStatus(projectDir);
188	      displayStatus(status, packageJson.version);
189	    } catch (error) {
190	      console.error(chalk.red('\nFailed to read status:'), error.message);
191	      process.exit(1);
192	    }
193	  },
194	};
195

1	/**
2	 * SKF Uninstall Command
3	 * Clean removal of SKF files, IDE skill directories, sidecar, and output folders.
4	 * Uses the manifest to know exactly what to remove.
5	 */
6	
7	const chalk = require('chalk');
8	const path = require('node:path');
9	const fs = require('fs-extra');
10	const { confirm, isCancel, spinner, log, outro } = require('@clack/prompts');
11	const { readManifest, MANIFEST_DIR, MANIFEST_FILE } = require('../lib/manifest');
12	const { removeAllSkfSkills } = require('../lib/ide-skills');
13	
14	/**
15	 * Count files that still exist on disk from manifest lists.
16	 */
17	async function countExistingFiles(projectDir, manifest) {
18	  const allFiles = [
19	    ...(manifest.files.skf || []),
20	    ...(manifest.files.sidecar || []),
21	    ...(manifest.files.ide_skills || []),
22	    ...(manifest.files.learning || []),
23	    ...(manifest.files.output || []),
24	  ];
25	
26	  let existing = 0;
27	  for (const file of allFiles) {
28	    if (await fs.pathExists(path.join(projectDir, file))) {
29	      existing++;
30	    }
31	  }
32	  return { total: allFiles.length, existing };
33	}
34	
35	/**
36	 * Display what will be removed, grouped by category.
37	 */
38	async function displayRemovalPlan(projectDir, manifest) {
39	  const categories = [
40	    { key: 'skf', label: 'SKF module files', dir: manifest.skf_folder },
41	    { key: 'sidecar', label: 'Agent sidecar state', dir: '_bmad/_memory/forger-sidecar' },
42	    { key: 'ide_skills', label: 'IDE skill directories' },
43	    { key: 'learning', label: 'Learning material', dir: '_skf-learn' },
44	    { key: 'output', label: 'Output folder scaffolding' },
45	  ];
46	
47	  const lines = [];
48	  for (const cat of categories) {
49	    const files = manifest.files[cat.key] || [];
50	    if (files.length === 0) continue;
51	
52	    // Count how many still exist
53	    let existCount = 0;
54	    for (const f of files) {
55	      if (await fs.pathExists(path.join(projectDir, f))) existCount++;
56	    }
57	
58	    if (existCount === 0) continue;
59	
60	    if (cat.dir) {
61	      lines.push(`${chalk.red('×')} ${cat.label} ${chalk.dim(`(${cat.dir}/ — ${existCount} files)`)}`);
62	    } else {
63	      lines.push(`${chalk.red('×')} ${cat.label} ${chalk.dim(`(${existCount} files)`)}`);
64	    }
65	  }
66	
67	  // Manifest itself
68	  lines.push(`${chalk.red('×')} Installation manifest ${chalk.dim(`(${MANIFEST_DIR}/${MANIFEST_FILE})`)}`);
69	
70	  log.warn(`The following will be removed:\n${lines.join('\n')}`);
71	}
72	
73	/**
74	 * Remove files listed in the manifest, then clean empty parent directories.
75	 */
76	async function removeFiles(projectDir, fileList) {
77	  let removed = 0;
78	  for (const file of fileList) {
79	    const fullPath = path.join(projectDir, file);
80	    if (await fs.pathExists(fullPath)) {
81	      await fs.remove(fullPath);
82	      removed++;
83	    }
84	  }
85	  return removed;
86	}
87	
88	/**
89	 * Remove a directory if it exists and is empty (or force remove).
90	 */
91	async function removeEmptyDir(dirPath) {
92	  if (!(await fs.pathExists(dirPath))) return;
93	  try {
94	    const entries = await fs.readdir(dirPath);
95	    if (entries.length === 0) {
96	      await fs.remove(dirPath);
97	    }
98	  } catch {
99	    // ignore
100	  }
101	}
102	
103	module.exports = {
104	  command: 'uninstall',
105	  description: 'Remove SKF installation from the current project',
106	  options: [],
107	  action: async () => {
108	    try {
109	      const projectDir = process.cwd();
110	      const manifest = await readManifest(projectDir);
111	
112	      if (!manifest) {
113	        // Check if SKF exists without manifest
114	        const skfExists = await fs.pathExists(path.join(projectDir, '_bmad/skf'));
115	        if (skfExists) {
116	          log.warn(
117	            'No manifest found. Reinstall first to generate one,\nthen run uninstall again for clean removal.\nRun: npx bmad-module-skill-forge install',
118	          );
119	        } else {
120	          log.warn('SKF is not installed in this directory.');
121	        }
122	        process.exit(0);
123	        return;
124	      }
125	
126	      const { existing } = await countExistingFiles(projectDir, manifest);
127	      if (existing === 0) {
128	        log.warn('No SKF files found to remove.');
129	        // Clean up stale manifest
130	        await fs.remove(path.join(projectDir, MANIFEST_DIR, MANIFEST_FILE));
131	        process.exit(0);
132	        return;
133	      }
134	
135	      await displayRemovalPlan(projectDir, manifest);
136	
137	      const shouldProceed = await confirm({
138	        message: 'Proceed with uninstall?',
139	        initialValue: false,
140	      });
141	
142	      if (isCancel(shouldProceed) || !shouldProceed) {
143	        log.warn('Uninstall cancelled.');
144	        process.exit(0);
145	        return;
146	      }
147	
148	      const s = spinner();
149	
150	      // Remove IDE skill directories from all known platforms (skills + legacy command files)
151	      s.start('Removing IDE skill directories...');
152	      const removedIdeDirs = await removeAllSkfSkills(projectDir);
153	      if (removedIdeDirs.length > 0) {
154	        s.stop(`Cleaned skills from ${removedIdeDirs.length} IDE target(s)`);
155	      } else {
156	        s.stop('No IDE skill directories found');
157	      }
158	
159	      // Remove learning material directory
160	      const learnFiles = manifest.files.learning || [];
161	      if (learnFiles.length > 0) {
162	        s.start('Removing learning material...');
163	        const learnDir = path.join(projectDir, '_skf-learn');
164	        if (await fs.pathExists(learnDir)) {
165	          await fs.remove(learnDir);
166	        }
167	        s.stop('Learning material removed');
168	      }
169	
170	      // Remove output folder scaffolding (.gitkeep only)
171	      const outputFiles = manifest.files.output || [];
172	      if (outputFiles.length > 0) {
173	        s.start('Removing output scaffolding...');
174	        await removeFiles(projectDir, outputFiles);
175	        // Remove empty output folders
176	        for (const folder of [manifest.skills_output_folder, manifest.forge_data_folder]) {
177	          if (folder) {
178	            await removeEmptyDir(path.join(projectDir, folder));
179	          }
180	        }
181	        s.stop('Output scaffolding removed');
182	      }
183	
184	      // Remove sidecar
185	      s.start('Removing agent sidecar...');
186	      const sidecarDir = path.join(projectDir, '_bmad', '_memory', 'forger-sidecar');
187	      if (await fs.pathExists(sidecarDir)) {
188	        await fs.remove(sidecarDir);
189	      }
190	      await removeEmptyDir(path.join(projectDir, '_bmad', '_memory'));
191	      s.stop('Agent sidecar removed');
192	
193	      // Remove SKF module directory
194	      s.start('Removing SKF module...');
195	      const skfDir = path.join(projectDir, manifest.skf_folder);
196	      if (await fs.pathExists(skfDir)) {
197	        await fs.remove(skfDir);
198	      }
199	      s.stop('SKF module removed');
200	
201	      // Remove manifest
202	      const manifestPath = path.join(projectDir, MANIFEST_DIR, MANIFEST_FILE);
203	      if (await fs.pathExists(manifestPath)) {
204	        await fs.remove(manifestPath);
205	      }
206	      // Clean empty _bmad/_config/ and _bmad/ if we were the only occupant
207	      await removeEmptyDir(path.join(projectDir, MANIFEST_DIR));
208	      await removeEmptyDir(path.join(projectDir, '_bmad'));
209	
210	      outro('SKF uninstalled successfully.');
211	
212	      process.exit(0);
213	    } catch (error) {
214	      console.error(chalk.red('\nUninstall failed:'), error.message);
215	      process.exit(1);
216	    }
217	  },
218	};
219

chore(release): apply code-review patches (Story 5.1)

Five patches from the Story 5.1 code review on commits 540fdda +
7b84411 (three parallel adversarial layers — Blind Hunter / Edge
Case Hunter / Acceptance Auditor). Acceptance Auditor reported
22/22 AC compliance; this commit resolves the two decision-needed
findings plus three patch findings raised during triage.

- docs/v1.0.0-launch-audit.md → release-audits/v1.0.0-launch-audit.md
  (renamed). The audit is a process artifact (internal backlog IDs,
  sign-off placeholder, `_bmad-output/` cross-refs), not contract
  documentation — it did not belong next to STABILITY.md /
  RELEASING.md in the Starlight-published `docs/` folder. Now
  tracked at repo root outside the content collection. Resolves
  broken `_bmad-output/…` links on the published site and the
  github-slugger dotted-filename slug mangling (`v1.0.0-launch-audit`
  → `v100-launch-audit`) for the backlink from RELEASING.md.
  Relative `../_bmad-output/…` links inside the audit still resolve
  since `release-audits/` sits at the same level as `docs/`.

- docs/RELEASING.md § Cutting v1.0.0-rc.1: backlink to the audit
  updated to an absolute GitHub URL (audit now outside content
  collection; relative link would 404 on Starlight). Separately,
  `git add package.json` → `git add package.json package-lock.json`
  in the CLI block — `npm version` bumps both files, committing
  only the manifest leaves the lockfile out of sync and breaks
  `npm ci` at release time.

- release-audits/v1.0.0-launch-audit.md: matrix evidence refreshed.
  Row 1 now cites `## Covered Surfaces` H2 at STABILITY.md:10 per
  AC 4 verbatim (previously only cited banner :6). Row 2 CHANGELOG
  range 7–64 → 7–67 (verified via grep: `## [0.10.0]` at line 68).
  Row 10 scenario H3 line numbers 196/225/252/273/305/362/423 →
  196/228/255/279/311/368/429 (post-R4/R5/R6 inserts; the original
  numbers were captured pre-insert-time).

- tools/cli/commands/update.js: H1-equivalent silent-exit-0 bug.
  Sibling sweep of all four CLI commands verified the Story 5.1
  Dev Notes premise "others already exit 1 on failure paths" was
  wrong re: update.js — unconditional `process.exit(0)` at function
  tail regardless of `result.success`. `status.js` and `uninstall.js`
  are clean (no `installer.install` consumption / all exits
  explicit). Patch mirrors the install.js AC 10 shape: success
  branch retains displaySuccess + exit(0); new else branch
  stderr-routes 'Update failed.' and exits 1. Expands effective
  scope from Story 5.1's stated 7-file envelope to 8 files — AC 1
  verbatim forbade changes to tools/cli/commands/{update,status,
  uninstall}.js on the recon premise; the premise was wrong.

Review also deferred 11 findings (mostly tooling / IA polish for
post-v1.0.0) and dismissed 20 (noise or verified safe, including
one Blind Hunter false positive where a blockquote's pre-existing
2-space indent already bound it to the list item as continuation).
See _bmad-output/implementation-artifacts/5-1-pre-v1-0-0-readiness-audit.md
§ Review Findings for full disposition.

Verification: sibling sweep across all four CLI commands
(install/update/status/uninstall); scenario line numbers
re-verified via grep (196/228/255/279/311/368/429); CHANGELOG
range verified via grep (`## [0.10.0]` at :68); audit rename
confirmed via git diff --cached --stat.

Resolves\n  broken _bmad-output/ links on the published site and the\n  github-slugger dotted-filename slug mangling (v1.0.0-launch-audit\n  → v100-launch-audit) for the backlink from RELEASING.md.\n  Relative ../_bmad-output/ links inside the audit still resolve\n  since release-audits/ sits at the same level as docs/.\n\n- docs/RELEASING.md § Cutting v1.0.0-rc.1: backlink to the audit\n  updated to an absolute GitHub URL (audit now outside content\n  collection; relative link would 404 on Starlight). Separately,\n  git add package.json → git add package.json package-lock.json\n  in the CLI block — npm version bumps both files, committing\n  only the manifest leaves the lockfile out of sync and breaks\n  npm ci at release time.\n\n- release-audits/v1.0.0-launch-audit.md: matrix evidence refreshed.\n  Row 1 now cites ## Covered Surfaces H2 at STABILITY.md:10 per\n  AC 4 verbatim (previously only cited banner :6). Row 2 CHANGELOG\n  range 7–64 → 7–67 (verified via grep: ## [0.10.0] at line 68).\n  Row 10 scenario H3 line numbers 196/225/252/273/305/362/423 →\n  196/228/255/279/311/368/429 (post-R4/R5/R6 inserts; the original\n  numbers were captured pre-insert-time).\n\n- tools/cli/commands/update.js: H1-equivalent silent-exit-0 bug.\n  Sibling sweep of all four CLI commands verified the Story 5.1\n  Dev Notes premise 'others already exit 1 on failure paths' was\n  wrong re: update.js — unconditional process.exit(0) at function\n  tail regardless of result.success. status.js and uninstall.js\n  are clean (no installer.install consumption / all exits\n  explicit). Patch mirrors the install.js AC 10 shape: success\n  branch retains displaySuccess + exit(0); new else branch\n  stderr-routes 'Update failed.' and exits 1.
